The pressure-precursor inference is falsified if refinement removes or reverses the monotone rise before the same boundary, or if root-complete candidates repeatedly cross $P_{\mathrm{root}}=1$ without a corresponding change in the declared tolerance/enclosure contract. The cadence- reversal inference is falsified if independent tighter histories do not reproduce the turn and current handoff, or if a complete return requires permanently same-sense circulation after all.

The strongest geometry inference is now narrower and more useful: F6c carries one exact body-fixed axial current channel, but its magnitude is jointly made by circulation and breathing shear. Its polarity-odd internal coordinates can grow while centroid and dipole remain zero. A standard-model braid search should therefore seek periodic or recurrent histories in this coupled six-coordinate system, not insist on fixed-radius architrino orbits. Frequency becomes a return property of the complete delayed geometry; simple orbital frequency is only one possible component.

#### F6c Charge-Conjugation Coordinates

Use a continuous lifted phase on one declared root-valid branch rather than averaging wrapped angles. For each collective coordinate $z\in\{h,\rho,\theta\}$, define

$$
z_{\mathrm{even}}
=
\frac12(z_++z_-),
\qquad
z_{\mathrm{odd}}
=
\frac12(z_+-z_-).
$$

The geometry-level conjugation operation exchanges the polarity sectors:

$$
\mathcal C:
\left(
z_{\mathrm{even}},z_{\mathrm{odd}}
\right)
\longmapsto
\left(
z_{\mathrm{even}},-z_{\mathrm{odd}}
\right).
$$

This separates two jobs without changing the eight-identity inventory:

| Coordinate class | Candidate geometric job | Required conjugation behavior |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $h_{\mathrm{even}},\rho_{\mathrm{even}},\theta_{\mathrm{even}}$ | scalar envelope, common cadence, and charge-conjugate shared response | invariant |
| $h_{\mathrm{odd}},\rho_{\mathrm{odd}},\theta_{\mathrm{odd}}$ | polarity-differential exposure, internal electric deformation, and conjugate branch label | sign reversing |

An effective charge projection $Q_{\mathrm{eff}}$ built from this geometry must be odd:

$$
Q_{\mathrm{eff}}
\left(
\mathbf z_{\mathrm{even}},-\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}
\right)
=
-Q_{\mathrm{eff}}
\left(
\mathbf z_{\mathrm{even}},\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}
\right),
$$

while a scalar envelope or mass-facing projection must be even. The coefficients and functional form are not inserted here; they must be computed from the same root-resolved wake-exposure record.

Plainly: equal positive and negative inventory can still expose charge if the two sectors occupy different retained geometries. Swapping those geometries reverses the exposed sign while preserving the shared envelope. This supplies a native architecture for a charge-conjugate pair without adding or removing architrinos.

The resulting branch hypotheses are ordered but unassigned:

1. a return with $\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}=\mathbf0$ is a neutral-braid candidate;
2. two retained returns with $\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}=\pm\mathbf z_*$ and the same even coordinates are a charge-conjugate pair candidate; and
3. a weakly exposed neutral branch may have nonzero odd internal motion whose complete-cycle charge projection still vanishes.

The second row has the structural shape needed by an electron/positron pair, but no such assignment is licensed until the branches retain, their $Q_{\mathrm{eff}}$ values are equal and opposite, their scalar projections agree, and their moment/spin correlation transforms correctly. The first and third rows may be relevant to Noether sea or neutral-lepton geometry, but neutrality alone does not distinguish those roles.

There is nevertheless an exact field-free conjugate-trajectory theorem at the Master Equation level. Apply global polarity inversion to one complete history,

$$
q_a\longmapsto-q_a
\qquad\text{for every persistent architrino }a,
$$

without changing its initial geometric paths. Every ordered interaction factor is invariant:

$$
(-q_t)(-q_r)=q_tq_r.
$$

Causal roots depend only on the paths, and both partner and self-hit acceleration contributions retain the same polarity product. Therefore the field-free acceleration history is unchanged. Wherever ordinary evolution is unique on the shared complete-root branch, the conjugated record follows the same unlabeled geometric paths. Relabeling those paths by their new polarity exchanges the two F6c sectors, so

$$
\mathbf z_{\mathrm{even}}^{\mathcal C}(T)
=
\mathbf z_{\mathrm{even}}(T),
\qquad
\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}^{\mathcal C}(T)
=
-\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}(T).
$$

The polarity-weighted current moment also reverses:

$$
\mathbf m_{\mathrm{cur}}^{\mathcal C}(T)
=
-\mathbf m_{\mathrm{cur}}(T).
$$

Plainly: the primitive law supplies an exact way for a particle candidate and its antiparticle candidate to have identical field-free shape and recurrence, opposite polarity-odd internal geometry, and opposite signed current moment. No second interaction law is needed. This theorem still does not say whether F6c is charged, neutral, electron-like, nucleon-like, or retained; that depends on the missing effective charge and mass-facing projections.

An external source is not part of this theorem unless it is conjugated too. Holding a source assembly fixed while conjugating only the receiver changes source--receiver polarity products. Equal-and-opposite effective response in that controlled comparison is therefore a recovery obligation for the $E0$--$E4$ experiment, not something established by the field-free symmetry alone.

In the $E0$--$E4$ electric-response experiment, these coordinates give an immediate readout. A neutral receiver may have zero group translation while the source drives $\mathbf z_{\mathrm{odd}}$ and directional off-manifold deformations; conjugate charged receivers must share the even deformation and reverse both group response and odd-sector response under complete source/receiver conjugation.

#### Tetrahedral Singlet--Triplet Deformation Chart

F6c's six collective coordinates describe only module-common motion. For any one module coordinate $z_{i\sigma}$, write

$$
z_{i\sigma}
=
\bar z_\sigma
+
\delta z_{i\sigma},
\qquad
\bar z_\sigma
=
\frac14\sum_i z_{i\sigma},
\qquad
\sum_i\delta z_{i\sigma}=0.
$$

The four-module permutation space splits exactly into one common direction and one three-dimensional sum-zero space:

$$
\mathbb R^4
=
\operatorname{span}\{(1,1,1,1)\}
\mathbin{\oplus}
\left\{
\boldsymbol\delta:\sum_i\delta_i=0
\right\}.
$$

The tetrahedral axes turn that abstract triplet into a physical body-frame vector:

$$
\mathbf Z_{z,\sigma}
=
\sum_i
\delta z_{i\sigma}\hat{\mathbf n}_i.
$$

Using $\hat{\mathbf n}_i\mathbin{\cdot}\hat{\mathbf n}_j=-1/3$ for $i\ne j$ and $\sum_i\delta z_{i\sigma}=0$ gives

$$
\left\|
\mathbf Z_{z,\sigma}
\right\|^2
=
\frac43
\sum_i
\delta z_{i\sigma}^2,
$$

and the inverse is

$$
\delta z_{i\sigma}
=
\frac34
\hat{\mathbf n}_i
\mathbin{\cdot}
\mathbf Z_{z,\sigma}.
$$

Thus no directional information is lost: the three independent departures from common tetrahedral breathing are exactly equivalent to one body-frame vector.

Plainly: if one side of the braid is stretched more than the others, the four module changes encode a unique arrow in space. Equal changes are scalar breathing; sum-zero changes are directional polarization. The tetrahedron supplies both in the smallest four-module package.

Conjugation now produces even and odd directional triplets:

$$
\mathbf Z_{z,\mathrm{even}}
=
\frac12
\left(
\mathbf Z_{z,+}+\mathbf Z_{z,-}
\right),
\qquad
\mathbf Z_{z,\mathrm{odd}}
=
\frac12
\left(
\mathbf Z_{z,+}-\mathbf Z_{z,-}
\right).
$$

This suggests a compact sea-braid response chart:

| Native F6c response coordinate | Effective role to test | Required control |
| --- | --- | --- |
| common even singlets $\bar h_{\mathrm{even}},\bar\rho_{\mathrm{even}},\bar\theta_{\mathrm{even}}$ | scalar loading, common cadence, isotropic envelope response | source-direction randomization leaves the leading row unchanged |
| common odd singlets $\bar h_{\mathrm{odd}},\bar\rho_{\mathrm{odd}},\bar\theta_{\mathrm{odd}}$ | charge-facing exposure imbalance or conjugate branch coordinate | complete conjugation reverses the row |
| odd polar triplets $\mathbf Z_{z,\mathrm{odd}}$ | electric-like internal polarization and directional sea strain | source polarity reversal and spatial reflection give the declared polar transformation |
| circulation-weighted axial triplets | magnetic-like orientation/current response | circulation reversal and parity distinguish the axial row from the polar row |

Under proper tetrahedral rotations, polar and axial triplets have the same three-dimensional rotation behavior. They separate only when the retained record also carries reflection/parity and circulation history. Therefore the triplet dimension alone cannot distinguish effective electric and magnetic response.

For the same reason, this triplet is not a color assignment. It currently rotates as a body-frame spatial vector. Calling its three components colors would alias an internal gauge label with laboratory orientation unless a separate quotient or compensated internal operation is derived.

The measured zero within-sector normal fraction on the unperturbed circular history says the isolated source does not spontaneously excite the triplet at that row. A directional external source is expected to do so. That expectation must be evaluated from its actual root-resolved acceleration pattern; no primitive $\mathbf E$ or $\mathbf B$ is applied to an architrino.

Claim grade: the $1\mathbin{\oplus}3$ decomposition, norm identity, and inverse map are exact tetrahedral algebra. Their use as scalar, electric-polar, and magnetic-axial response channels is inferred. Falsifier: a root-resolved directional source cannot be represented by the triplet, complete conjugation or reflection violates the declared transformation parity, or an accepted candidate recovers the same response with a lower-dimensional native record.

#### Three Charge-Preserving Coordinates, Not Yet Three Modes

Suppose a retained F6c charge branch fixes its conjugation-odd exposure basin. Its instantaneous charge-preserving collective configuration tangent is

$$
\mathcal E_{\mathrm{even}}
=
\operatorname{span}
\left\{
\delta h_{\mathrm{even}},
\delta\rho_{\mathrm{even}},
\delta\theta_{\mathrm{even}}
\right\}.
$$

This three-dimensional configuration space does **not** by itself imply three dynamical modes. The native state also contains the three coordinate rates and the retained causal history. A delayed second-order return derivative acts on that larger state and may have more than three admissible modes.

An exactly-three opportunity appears only if ordinary evolution supplies an invariant graph $\iota$ that slaves the charge-preserving rates and relevant history to these three coordinates on a declared return section. With projection $P_{\mathrm{even}}$ and full native return map $\mathcal R$, the reduced operator would be

$$
M_{\mathrm{even}}^{\mathrm{red}}
=
D\!\left(
P_{\mathrm{even}}
\mathbin{\circ}
\mathcal R
\mathbin{\circ}
\iota
\right).
$$

Only after proving that this is a closed $3\times3$ operator does the characteristic equation become cubic:

$$
\det
\left(
M_{\mathrm{even}}^{\mathrm{red}}-\lambda I_3
\right)
=0.
$$

Conditional on that closure, axial breathing, transverse breathing, and phase circulation can mix into at most three reduced eigenvectors. Unlike the three tetrahedral partitions, these are internal deformation directions rather than three rotated laboratory axes.

Plainly: F6c supplies three promising shape directions, but position, velocity, and memory can support additional modes. Three generations may correspond to three repeatable charge-preserving oscillations only if the full delayed dynamics collapses onto a genuine three-coordinate return law. Counting three coordinate names is not that proof.

Using these modes for the observed generation triplet requires every row below to close on one retained record:

1. the invariant graph $\iota$ exists, is attractive or otherwise dynamically selected, and carries all required retained-history information;
2. $M_{\mathrm{even}}^{\mathrm{red}}$ has exactly three distinct admissible long-lived or resonance modes on the accepted branch;
3. all three retain the same conjugation-odd exposure basin, effective charge, moment/spin transformation row, and interaction representation;
4. their return cadence, action, exposure, and exit-corridor differences recover the mass, lifetime, and reaction hierarchy without fitting a generation label;
5. every rate, memory, odd-sector, or off-manifold mode outside the reduced triplet is a charge-changing branch, transient, confined mode, or otherwise correctly dispositioned; and
6. decay of a higher mode to a lower mode closes architrino identity, emitted carriers, recoil, wake, and boundary flux on one event ledger.

The weak-facing projection must also avoid the earlier commuting-operator no-go. If $W_{\mathrm{even}}$ is the weak exposure/measurement operator on the same reduced subspace, nontrivial mixing requires

$$
\left[
M_{\mathrm{even}}^{\mathrm{red}},W_{\mathrm{even}}
\right]
\ne0.
$$

The required independent posture can come from how a weak source couples differently to axial breathing, transverse breathing, and phase circulation, rather than from an inserted generation matrix.

Claim grade: the three-dimensional instantaneous configuration tangent is derived. The invariant graph, closed $3\times3$ return operator, exactly-three generation interpretation, and noncommuting weak projection are inferred targets, not results. Any electron/muon/tau, neutrino, or quark assignment is guessed. Falsifier: no invariant reduction exists, the full or reduced return spectrum has additional admissible same-representation modes, effective charge changes between the proposed modes, or the independently derived weak operator necessarily commutes with the return operator.

Claim grade: even/odd transformation parity is derived from the declared sector exchange. Using the odd coordinates as the carrier of effective charge is inferred. Neutral-braid, charged-lepton, and neutral-lepton roles are guessed. Falsifier: a retained charge-conjugate pair requires different even geometry, has equal rather than opposite root-resolved exposure, or a neutral branch cannot suppress its complete-cycle charge projection despite the declared conjugation symmetry.

Claim grade: sector-wise centroid/dipole cancellation, the acceleration decomposition, and current-axis alignment are derived for the declared F6c formulas. Tangency, coordinate projections, and harmonics are measured on the hashed F6b prescribed-history diagnostic; they are not an EOM evolution. F6c binding, recurrence, retention, spinor behavior, and particle identity are guessed. Falsifier: exact substitution breaks a stated identity, a faithful root-ledger replay produces a material within-sector normal acceleration, or ordinary evolution immediately leaves the six-coordinate manifold without a bounded return.

Claim grade: the tetrahedral vector and second-moment identities, dipole cancellation, and one-versus-three current-moment sum are mathematical derivations for the prescribed geometry. F6 binding, retention, scalar response, spinor visibility, generation, weak projection, and particle role are guessed. Falsifier: the exact vector identities fail, or every root-valid/clearance-valid realization of the tetrahedral paths necessarily loses the required dipole, moment, or three-port separation before ordinary evolution is attempted.

Claim grade: inevitable same-circle counterrotation crossings, the parallel- plane clearance formula, axial dipole cancellation, signed-current addition, and orthogonal-triad second-moment identity are derived for the declared prescribed geometry. F5 as a bound fermion core and its possible history, generation, charge, and weak roles are guessed. Falsifier: its declared geometric identities fail, or ordinary Master Equation evolution cannot maintain a root-valid retained neighborhood before any particle-facing projection is applied.

### Nonlocal, Material, And Radiation Controls

Three benchmark families prevent the local axial-state picture from becoming too narrow:

- Aharonov--Bohm requires path-history holonomy around a shielded source even when the local effective magnetic readout on the paths is null.
- Quantum Hall behavior requires a perturbation-stable collective invariant or equivalent edge/bulk basin structure at material scale; it is not direct evidence for a fundamental braid knot type.
- Synchrotron and related radiation require the curved charged branch, anisotropic sea state, emitted carrier, source depletion, recoil, wake, remnant, polarization, and cooling to close on one event ledger.

Together with circulation and mirror controls, these cases distinguish local axial order, nonlocal loop memory, collective material topology, and outgoing carrier production. None may be substituted for another merely because all are described with an observer-level $\mathbf B$.

Plainly: magnetism is constrained by more than how a compass-like object turns. Loop phase, robust edge transport, and radiation each test a different part of the same proposed source--history--sea architecture.

### Framed Causal-History Bundle

A compact joint test object for the strongest rows is

$$
\mathfrak B_W
=
\left(
G_W,
\{F_a\},
\{U_\gamma\},
\mu_W,
\mathcal L_W
\right),
$$

where $G_W$ is the identity-labeled active-root graph, $F_a\in SO(3)$ is an ordered assembly frame, $U_\gamma$ is path-history transport, $\mu_W$ is the finite-window basin measure, and $\mathcal L_W$ is the complete identity, polarity, action, energy, momentum, angular-momentum, wake, medium, remnant, and boundary ledger. A first certificate would test root transport, path concatenation, loop holonomy, $\mathbb Z_2$ lift parity, separatrix measure, and ledger closure on one record.

This bundle is a proposed certificate shape, not a claim that all components are independent or that one retained braid already realizes them.

Plainly: keep one labeled causal network, attach the orientation and path memory the experiments demand, and allow topology changes only when the same record explains exactly what changed and where every conserved quantity went.

## Ranked Geometry-Inference Summary

This table is a local research-priority ranking, not an equation maturity, acceptance, evidence, or retained-geometry score. Four integer coordinates run from $0$ to $4$: independent-owner breadth $I$, power to discriminate geometries $D$, proximity to native records $P$, and inverse-step uncertainty $U$, where larger $U$ means greater uncertainty. The score is

$$
S_{\mathrm{geom}}
=
8I+7D+6P+4(4-U),
\qquad
0\le S_{\mathrm{geom}}\le100.
$$

The score prioritizes requirements that are broadly supported, eliminate many candidates, attach closely to Master Equation or retained-branch records, and make a comparatively small inverse leap. Ties are intentional.

## Inventory Coverage And Gaps

The campaign traversed all 39 substantive equation packets and all 26 substantive benchmark cases. Every equation row contributed either a geometry constraint, a shared carrier or ledger obligation, a scale-separation rule, or a downstream compatibility check. `EQ-04A` remains a deliberately weak diagnostic until an independent charged-lepton mass map exists.

Five benchmark gaps should remain visible without creating premature queue objects:

1. no dedicated static/dynamic electric polarizability, Stark, neutral-gradient, or proton/neutron comparison packet exists; `BR-E-07` through `BR-E-10` and the merged $E0$--$E4$ extension hold those requirements until retained carriers exist;
2. no dedicated parity-violation, beta-decay, or muon-decay packet presently sharpens the weak-sector chirality inference; and
3. no dedicated confinement, hadron-spectroscopy, jet, deep-inelastic, or lattice benchmark exists in `mapping-benchmarks`; the Standard Model closure packet holds these observer-level constraints but no retained corridor simulation supplies their geometry;
4. no dedicated proton/neutron electric-and-magnetic form-factor, polarization-transfer, and elastic-to-inelastic packet intersects the spatial exposure rows needed to distinguish a nucleon envelope; and
5. no shared decay-angular-correlation packet presently compares parent frame, chirality, vector/scalar response, product helicity, and recoil across meson, baryon, weak-corridor, and scalar channels.

These are coverage gaps, not permission to infer their missing geometry from observer-level theory. Add a packet only when it protects a tested observable or accepted mathematical constraint and has a current carrier capable of answering it.

Plainly: the current map is broad enough to rank the geometry burdens, but its weakest sectors still lack the clean experiments that would distinguish one native architecture from another.

## Competing Realization Families

The following hypotheses remain live and mutually distinguishable:

| Hypothesis | Working picture | What would count against it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `H0` Direct-wake dominant | Effective fields arise mainly from coarse-grained source causal histories; sea changes are secondary dressing. | A resolved response persists or propagates only when collective sea degrees of freedom are allowed. |
| `H1` Polar/axial sea split | Electric-like behavior projects mainly from a polar sea moment and magnetic-like behavior from an axial circulation moment. | Correct $\mathbf E$ and $\mathbf B$ behavior follows with either moment absent, or the split fails observer-motion mixing. |
| `H2` Unified response object | Electric and magnetic fields are observer projections of one deeper antisymmetric or causal-history response object. | No one native object reproduces both parities, induction, frame mixing, and independent source reversals. |
| `H3` Source-history magnetic projection | Magnetic behavior is primarily an axial projection of organized source history, with the sea supplying constitutive propagation or screening. | Magnetic response requires a local axial sea state even after the full source history is retained. |
| `H4` Coupled matter--sea state | The smallest carrier is a matter assembly together with a near-to-extended sea reorganization; neither part alone closes the behavior. | Direct-only or frozen-sea controls reproduce the complete requirement bundle. |
| `H5` Distinct propagating carrier | Stationary field response and radiation share source ancestry but radiation requires a retained outgoing packet or collective propagating mode. | The same stationary response record propagates source-free with the required photon and energy ledgers without an additional carrier state. |

These hypotheses organize tests; none is an accepted constitutive law. More than one may apply in different regimes, but regime changes must be derived and cannot become a license for private retuning.

Plainly: the packet keeps several physical pictures alive until reversals, propagation, relaxation, and same-record comparisons separate them.

## Matter-State, Sea-State, And Scale Must Remain Distinct

Inverse mapping must keep four operations distinct: changing an observer-level scale coordinate, changing a persistent matter assembly, producing a transient event-level retuning, and changing the surrounding sea. None implies another without a derived relation.

For a matter transition $A\rightarrow B$, record separately:

- internal matter cadence, radii, envelope scale and shape, phase, exposure, and wake organization;
- near-sea retuning coupled to the matter transition;
- environmental and extended sea differences;
- event momentum transfer or other conventional comparison scale;
- effective electric and magnetic projections; and
- the final measured observable or inferred coupling.

This separation preserves four possibilities: a fixed-background projection, matter-state retuning, coupled local matter--sea retuning, and an extended sea-state difference. Agreement with a conventional running law constrains the combined projection but does not directly reveal which native coordinates changed.

Plainly: a higher experimental scale does not by itself prove a smaller or faster persistent electron braid. The physical event may alter the assembly, the nearby sea, both, or neither; the record must show which.

## Direct-Wake And Sea-Mediation Controls

For each candidate realization, evaluate at least these mathematical controls:

1. **Direct-only:** preserve the resolved source history while suppressing the authored collective sea response.
2. **Frozen-sea:** retain the reference sea record while changing the source history.
3. **Responsive-sea:** evolve or recompute the declared sea degrees of freedom with no coefficient change.
4. **Source-removed relaxation:** remove or reverse the maintaining source and track what relaxes, remains, or propagates away.
5. **Randomized orientation:** preserve scalar population data while destroying polar, axial, or alignment order.
6. **Polarity and circulation reversal:** change one signed native feature at a time while preserving the remaining geometry.
7. **Near/environment/far knockout:** suppress one spatial sea domain at a time to determine whether the response is local, environmental, extended, or a boundary artifact.
8. **Receiver substitution:** change the receiver class while holding the source, sea, carrier, and boundary record fixed.

An authored sea model swept over its own parameters is evidence about that model, not evidence that nature uses the model. These controls become physical evidence only when the candidate sea response is derived from resolved braid histories or compared with an independent instrument.

Plainly: the controls ask whether the source acts directly, through the sea, or through both, and whether the inferred field state exists independently of one specially chosen detector.

## Working Matrix Schema

Every developed requirement row should eventually contain:

| Column | Required content |
| --- | --- |
| Requirement ID | Stable identifier local to this packet |
| Effective behavior | Raw observable, equation term, or benchmark behavior without substrate interpretation |
| Provenance owner | Exact equation, benchmark, or corpus source that owns the constraint |
| Regime and tolerance | Domain in which the requirement applies and its declared numerical or logical tolerance |
| Transformation signature | Rotation, reflection, polarity, circulation, source, receiver, and history reversals |
| Deduced requirement | What every successful native realization must produce |
| Inferred native requirement | Stronger but nonunique implication for native structure |
| Source-assembly variables | Inventory, exposure, motion, internal circulation, cadence, phase, and retained history |
| Matter or receiver variables | Retained geometry, ordered frame, basin, exposure, response, and recoil |
| Near/environment/far sea variables | Density, cadence, flow, polar order, axial order, alignment, strain, memory, and spatial support |
| Carrier and boundary variables | Propagating packet, wake history, causal support, and incoming boundary record |
| Direct/sea disposition | Direct-only, sea-essential, mixed, or unresolved |
| Candidate realizations | Explicit geometries capable of attempting the row |
| Forward projection | Calculation from the native record to the effective behavior |
| Ledger | Identity, action, energy, momentum, angular momentum, wake, and sea updates |
| Controls and falsifier | Knockout, reversal, null, refinement, and exact failure condition |
| Evidence grade | Derived, measured, inferred, or guessed, with instrument and source identity |

Plainly: a mature row must say what was observed, who owns it, what native record is proposed, how the forward calculation works, and exactly what would falsify it. A prose resemblance is not enough.

The matrix should link to existing equation and benchmark owners rather than copy their complete formulas, histories, scores, or provenance records.

## Correct Work Order

Use this sequence for each new behavior family:

1. State the raw observer behavior without explaining it through a primitive field.
2. Extract magnitude, direction, covariance, symmetry, reversal, range, temporal, and conservation requirements.
3. Grade each statement as a deduced requirement or inferred native requirement.
4. Populate every relevant role in the absolute native record, including any sea domain whose contribution is presently unknown.
5. Enumerate the smallest candidate realization levels: one causal-root contribution, one retained matter braid, a local matter--sea complex, a collective sea or material response, and the effective observer projection.
6. Derive the effective output forward from each candidate.
7. Apply direct/sea, reversal, null, receiver, and refinement controls.
8. Intersect the surviving candidate sets across independent equations and benchmarks.
9. Call a geometry selected only after it is retained or otherwise accepted, passes the full requirement bundle, and survives comparison with competing realizations.

Simple devices or idealized situations may be introduced when a particular ambiguity needs a clean discriminator. They do not replace this work order and do not become new priority programs merely because they are easy to visualize.

Plainly: requirements first, candidates second, forward calculation third, selection last.

### Execution Frontier From The Current Clues

The following frontier is ordered by prerequisite, not by a scalar score. It separates work that can improve discovery now from tests that would be meaningless before a retained candidate exists.

| Phase | Work that can reduce uncertainty | Product | What remains barred |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| now: candidate generation | encode the non-aliasing and contradiction-core rows as early candidate filters; require persistent identities, sufficient frame/history coordinates, declared exposure maps, and no immediate overgeneration | smaller initial-condition and morphology families before expensive evolution | particle labels, fitted masses, or prescribed recurrence may not rescue a candidate |
| now: discovery instrumentation | extend the existing attractor census rather than creating another analyzer, adding RMS scale, trace-free shape, ordered frame, recurrence depths, return permutation, cross-phases, and successful root identities where the public contract permits | one common motion fingerprint for every search candidate | no topology, spinor, or retention claim from centroid/separation alone |
| now: exact implementation controls | construct complete-history proper-rotation, global-polarity, and mirror sibling bundles with fixed law and root certificates | validated covariance instruments and identity maps | agreement between transformed runs is not an independent proof of the kernel or a particle geometry |
| continuing retained-core search | evolve bounded framed, multi-component families under ordinary EOM dynamics and apply identity, retention, motion, and topology stages before precision comparisons | first positive-width retained or metastable core with a complete history record | no linearized spectrum about an unbalanced or prescribed configuration |
| first retained core | build the retained-branch mode atlas, signed electromagnetic perturbation tomography, multi-probe exposure continuation, and complete $C/P$/rotation tests | independent native modes, response rank, transformation class, and first nonlinear boundaries | no Standard Model identity from one successful mode or response |
| first reusable family | compare same-inventory modes, charged-lepton relatives, proton/neutron, photon/vector/scalar roles, and formation-versus-exit ordering under one fixed law | evidence for or against a reusable core/decorations/corridor factorization | no private geometry, sea state, quotient, or clock for each particle |
| first certified product basins | execute allowed/null transition graphs, pair formation and inverse-event controls, branching/survival packets, and backreaction | reaction geometry with exact provenance and daughter-basin classification | no pair capture, annihilation reciprocity, or decay claim from conserved counts alone |
| first autonomous orientation/exchange apparatus | perform $2\pi/4\pi$, two-assembly exchange, Stern--Gerlach, polarization analyzer, contextuality, and pair-provenance tests | spinor, statistics, measurement, and relational-history evidence | no exchange or Bell claim from a static endpoint snapshot |
| precision stage | recover masses, action, couplings, running, line strengths, widths, form factors, and cross sections from the already fixed carrier family | quantitative selection among surviving architectures | no retuning of branch, exposure, response law, sea state, detector, or convention per observable |

Plainly: the immediate work is to prune candidate charts and improve the common motion record. Precision recovery becomes meaningful only after a retained core and then a reusable family actually exist.

The most actionable software-facing step is the common motion-fingerprint extension because it improves retained-core discovery and is reused by every later mode, field-response, symmetry, and spectroscopy test. The most actionable mathematical step is the candidate non-aliasing filter. The most important physical blocker remains the first positive-width retained core.

No queue lifecycle changes are made here. Promoting an implementation object requires the owning Braid Program or EOM workstream to accept the scope and consumer contract.

Plainly: improve what the search records now, use the new constraints to avoid bad candidates, and be ready to interrogate the first real retained braid from many directions. Do not wait until after discovery to decide what information the run should have preserved.

## Ownership And Non-Advancement Boundary

This file owns the cross-row inference method, requirement statements, candidate realization register, and same-record braid-role map. It does not own or alter:

- equation maturity scores or accepted evidence decisions;
- benchmark provenance or covariance;
- retained braid existence or stability;
- Master Equation closure;
- photon-gate acceptance;
- electron, positron, or charge-quantum derivations;
- the eventual Noether sea electromagnetic constitutive law; or
- reader-facing corpus claims.

Requirement statements in this packet do not advance `EMAP-001` through `EMAP-004`, `EQ-13`, `EQ-26A`, or `EQ-27`. A requirement row becomes evidence only when its owning source, retained record, forward derivation, independent comparison, and falsifier satisfy the relevant acceptance contract.

Plainly: this packet narrows the search and makes the proof obligations explicit. It cannot declare that the required geometry exists.

## 2026-08-26 Braid Evaluation Dispositions

The [all-candidate evaluation campaign](../braid-program/evidence/2026-08-26-all-candidate-evaluation-campaign-closeout.md) assigns `STASIS` to F1--F4 at their incomplete `H1` charts, `DEMOTED` to the declared F5 two-ring realization at its exact `H2` obstruction, `STASIS` to parent-only F6, `DEMOTED` to the F6b circular realization at its measured `H4` member-acceleration failure, and `STASIS` to F6c at `H5`. Both six-accessory continuations remain in stasis behind `ACC1` because no named base braid passes `H5`.

The B1/B1.1--B1.3 and C1--C6 charts also remain in `STASIS` at `H2`. Their exact `R1/R2` capability passes and failures do not adjudicate a universal hard gate. Historical C-family records remain ineligible for the current identities.

Plainly: the seed comparisons still narrow what each geometry can attempt, but only F5 and F6b carry realization-scoped rejection evidence. Every other row stops at a missing chart, current-identity hard-gate record, or retained return.

## Current Concrete Pass And Blocker

The requirement-mining pass now covers electric, magnetic, induction, radiation, moment, holonomy, material, multiplet covariance, additive provenance labels, cross-sector geometry, and accessory placement relative to a retained braid. The electric field-identity matrix, magnetic source--sea--receiver reduction, weak-response skew lemma, cross-sector framed causal-history bundle, multiplet-orbit test, accessory-placement and braid-response requirements, ranked summary, and [candidate-by-requirement adjudication](braid-candidate-requirement-adjudication.md) are all present at report grade. The adjudication covers every active registry row without converting unknowns into failures or introducing a weighted physical score. The six residual recommendations each have an owner or explicit prerequisite.

The fixed-law continuation instrument also evaluates a signed weak regime and its first native geometric transition while keeping pair capture and backreaction fail-closed. Its prescribed fixture is `candidate_only`; it is an implementation check, not physical electromagnetic evidence.

The single highest-value general evidence blocker is one accepted, EOM-evolved, retained common-history source--sea--receiver family capable of populating the field-identity and translation/deformation rows without coefficient or geometry retuning. After that branch exists:

1. evaluate direct-wake, sea-essential, and mixed electric dispositions;
2. estimate the magnetic velocity-response kernel and its symmetric and skew parts under the independent reversal matrix;
3. test closure of the root-resolved history two-chain and attribute every unmatched edge;
4. continue the same fixed law through the first certified transition; and
5. attempt pair capture or backreaction only if that same record supplies certified conjugate product basins and exact inventory partition.

Within the present geometry campaign, the completed nonlinear F6c return-map and radial-frequency continuation are seed evidence, not a return. The radial-frequency coordinate improved the best eligible reflected section RMS by only $0.781421\%$, below its predeclared 10% material gate, so that direction is closed without refinement. A later campaign must predeclare a materially different coordinated-turn variable or a matched guarded/full-causal-root comparison. Each candidate must still carry its discrete winding cell and module-permutation provenance, and any return must use one eligible event and exact direct or declared reflected action across position, rate, and retained history. A scalar recrossing remains degenerate, and quotient-history equivalence under a same-polarity permutation is not labeled-history return.

Plainly: searching harder along the same radial-frequency knob is not justified. The next F6c calculation must change the dynamical question, not merely widen or repeat the exhausted search.

The response-mode matrix, trapped-electron `D1` matrix, dedicated nucleon polarizability comparison, and internal-speed inference remain behind their declared prerequisites. No additional prescribed geometry sweep can remove the retained-branch blocker.

The broader Standard Model inverse-geometry campaign remains productive even while that physical-evidence blocker stands. It now supplies a reusable-motif factorization, controlled particle comparisons, a transition graph, separate survival/coherence/linewidth rows, causal-versus-spatial topology abstention rules, distinct topological/basin/barrier protections, exposed-multipole non-aliasing, relational/exchange and apparatus-context requirements, scattering-boundary grammar, explicit contradiction cores, role-minimum information bundles, electromagnetic perturbation tomography, a post-retention mode-atlas protocol, atomic colored-transition constraints, and boson response-rank tests, as well as an ordered candidate-elimination sequence. The exact same-inventory $\pi/\rho$ and nucleon/$\Delta$ pairs now eliminate inventory-only hadron morphologies, but the corpus lacks a substantive multi-rung Regge, radial, orbital, or opposite-parity excitation inventory. Those deductions do not create a retained branch, but they reduce which branches are worth searching and identify the measurements that can reject them before precision fitting.

Plainly: the physical proof still needs one real retained evolution. Until it exists, inverse mapping can continue narrowing the search by forcing related particles and channels to reuse the same geometry motifs and by removing every candidate that aliases distinct clocks, transformations, exposures, or protections.

Closure goal: infer the smallest shared source, matter, sea, carrier, receiver, and boundary braid requirements capable of producing effective electric and magnetic behavior, then eliminate candidate geometries through existing equation and benchmark constraints before attempting a selected architecture.
